_softmax(z): z centered? Use stable.
```
def softmax(z):
  z=np.asarray(z); z=z-z.max(); e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); return e/s if finite else uniform
```

def score_p(p,n,fs): b=conv; m=b.max(); return 2.0*n*m
```

```
def cc(q,p,fs2): ar=p[::-1]; c=irfft(rfft(ar,fs2)*rfft(q,fs2),fs2); return c[n-1:2*n-1]
```

Weights:
```
def make_weights(b, mode, frac, rng, n):
  m=b.max()
  if mode soft: tau=max(1e-12, frac*m); e=np.exp(np.clip((b-m)/tau,-50,50)); return e/e.sum()
  topK: K=max(1,int(round(frac*(2*n-1)))); idx=np.argpartition(b,-K)[-K:]; e=np.exp((b[idx]-m)/max(tau...)); q zeros fill.
```
 But soft over all lags with tau=frac*m. For exact hard, frac small. We can choose frac adapt e.g. 0.15,0.08,0.03.

Direction:
```
def tangent_dir(q,p):
  c=cc(q,p,fs2)
  u=p*(c - float(p@c))
  norm=np.linalg.norm(u, ord=np.inf) or max
  if norm<1e-14: return None
  return u/norm  # for z -= alpha*u
```
 z update: z_new = z - alpha*dir_u? Since u has sum? p@u = mean*p? Actually sum p_i u_i= p@c - (p@c)*(p@p)=0, so tangent. Good. We'll store z centered maybe.

Line search:
```
def line_search_p(z, dir_u, p, current_score, base_alpha, max_evals, rng):
  # z basis current p. Try alphas.
  best_alpha=0; best_z=z; best_s=current_score
  # adapt scale: alpha list from scale* [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8]
  # Also maybe alpha=scale*2 if previous best at high
  for a in alphas:
    zc=z - a*dir_u
    pc=softmax(zc)
    s=score(pc)
    if s<best_s: best_s=s; best_alpha=a; best_z=zc
  return best_alpha>0, best_z, best_s, best_alpha
```

def run_job(job, end, deadline):
  while time < end and < deadline-0.2:
    p=softmax(z)
    b=conv(p)
    m=b.max(); s=2n*m
    # if s improves global best, update
    if s < global_best: update global; job.best_z=z; job.best_s=s; stuck=0
    # choose weights
    r=rng.random()
    if r<0.2: mode='top'; frac=current_frac (decrease with improvements)
    elif r<0.7: mode='soft'; frac=current_frac_soft
    else: mode='random_active'...
    q=weights(b,...)
    dir_u=tangent_dir(q,p)
    if dir_u None: reset z=best+noise; continue
    ok,znew,snew,frac_best=line_search(...)
    if snew < global_best: accept z=znew; update; scale adapt; stuck=0
    else: # maybe accept non-improvement to explore? We can keep z if soft improved? To avoid wandering, only accept exact improvements; but can accept if snew <= s + 1e-8? Maybe not.
      # if no improvement, occasionally accept small noise step? Better not; use random restart later.
      stuck+=1
      # adapt frac: if stuck > 5, change frac; if >30 random perturb; if >100 reset.
      if stuck>20: z = best_z + noise(0.3)
      if stuck>60: reset friction
    # LP move with low probability if n <=512 and time left:
    if (stuck>10 and job.lp_count<lp_max and time < deadline-30 and rng.random()<0.05):
       do_lp()
```

def apply_p_delta(p, delta, max_alpha=1.5):
  base_s=current_score
  best=p, base_s
  for a in [0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.25?]
     q=p + a*delta
     if min<0: q=np.maximum(q,0); q=q/q.sum()
     s=score(q)
     if s<best: update
```

def smooth_random_n(n, rng, smooth=0.5):

```
def smooth_noise(n, rng, length=None):
  g=rng.normal(size=n)
  w=max(3, int(n/20))
  k=np.ones(w)/w
  s=np.convolve(g,k,mode='same')
  # maybe if n large use FFT
  s-=s.mean()
  return s
```

Weights generation:
```
def make_weights(b, frac, rng, mode):
  m=b.max()
  if m<=0: uniform
  if mode == 'soft':
    tau=max(1e-15, frac*m)
    e=np.exp((b-m)/tau); e/=e.sum()
  elif 'top':
    rows=b.size
    K=max(1, min(rows, int(round(frac*rows))))
    if frac<=1: idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:] maybe if K==rows all.
    e=np.zeros(rows); e[idx]=1
    # Maybe weight by tangents: values b[idx]
    # To emphasize near max, use e[idx]=np.exp((b[idx]-m)/(tau_active))
    tau_act=max(1e-15, 0.05*m)
    val=np.exp((b[idx]-m)/tau_act); e[idx]=val; e/=e.sum()
  elif 'random_active':
    K=random int between... choose idx = np.argwhere(b> m - margin?)
```

def line_search_delta(p, delta, score, bmax, fs, deadline):

  # possible alpha max from negative

  alpha_max = inf

    if q.min()<0: q=np.maximum(q,0)
    s=q.sum(); if s<1e-12: continue

    if s2<best_s: best...
  return best_s<score, best_p, best_s, best_alpha

FFT conv:
```
def _convolve(p, fs):
    n=p.size
    F=rfft(p, fs)
    c=irfft(F*F, fs)
    c[:2*n-1] = np.maximum(c[:2*n-1], 0.0)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
Potential real rounding; if p all zero? not.

def _analytic_p(n, kind, rng, parent=None):
 t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
 eps=1e-6
 if kind==0: a=np.ones(n)
 elif kind==1 and parent is not None: a=np.interp(...)
 elif kind==2: a=(1+np.arange(n))**(-0.5)
 elif kind==3: reversed
 elif kind==4: a=(t+eps)**alpha where alpha=rng.choice([-0.5,-0.35,-0.2,-0.05,0.1,0.3]) maybe
 elif kind==5: a=np.exp(-gamma*t) gamma=rng.uniform(0.3,3)
 elif kind==6: a=np.exp(-gamma*(1-t))
 elif kind==7: f=1+int(rng.uniform(1,5)); ph=rng.uniform(0,2π); amp=rng.uniform(0.1,0.8); a=1+amp*np.cos(2π f t+ph); maybe combine two cos.
 elif kind==8: smooth noise: start ones +0.8*smooth_noise
 elif kind==9: random mask low: a=1+(rng.random(n)-0.5)*?; or a=np.where(rng.random(n)<0.85,1,0.1)
 else uniform
 a=np.maximum(a,1e-4)
 # maybe add small smooth noise
 return norm
```

Time slicing:
```
def round_robin(jobs, end_time, hard_deadline, slice_sec=4.0, target_update=None):
   order=list(range(len(jobs)))
   while time.monotonic() < min(end_time, hard_deadline):
      for oi in order:
         if time.monotonic() >= min(end_time, hard_deadline): break
         t_stop = min(end_time, hard_deadline, time.monotonic()+slice_sec)
         job=jobs[oi]
         try: job.run_slice(t_stop, hard_deadline, target_update)
         except Exception: pass
```
Inside job run_slice checks time.

  focused.append(OptJob(best_n, best_p, seed+...))
  round_robin(focused, deadline-20,...)
```

def run_slice(self, end_time, hard_deadline, update):
    while time.monotonic() < min(end_time, hard_deadline)-0.05:
        self.it +=1
        # if current z far from best? We can set z=best_z at start of each slice? Let's maintain current.
        p = _softmax(self.z)
        b,s = self._score_conv(p)  # returns score,b
        if s < self.best_s -1e-12:
            self.best_p=p.copy(); self.best_s=s; update(p,s); self.stuck=0
        # If current worse than best by >0.05? reset to best+small noise
        if s > self.best_s + max(1e-4, 0.02*self.best_s):
            self.z = self._log_p(self.best_p) + 0.05*smooth_noise(...)
            continue
        # choose step
        do_lp = self.use_lp and self.lp_count < self.lp_max and (self.it % self.lp_interval == 0 or self.stuck > 8) and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline-25 and self.n<=640
        improved=False
        if do_lp:
            ok=self._lp_tangent_step(p,b)
            if ok: improved=True
        if not improved:
            ok=self._gradient_step(p,b)
            if ok: improved=True
        if not improved:
            # randomly perturb current, maybe evaluate and if improve global/local accept
            for _ in range(min(3, self.stuck))?
                z2 = self._perturb_z(self.z, sigma=...)
                p2=_softmax(z2); s2,_=self._score(p2)
                if s2 < self.best_s: update; self.best_p=p2; self.best_z=z2; self.z=z2; self.stuck=0; improved=True; break
                elif s2 < s:  # improve current
                    self.z=z2; self.stuck+=1
                    # maybe continue
            if not improved:
                self.stuck +=1
                if self.stuck > 40:
                    # reset to best with larger noise
                    self.z=self.best_z + smooth_noise(0.6)
                # adapt frac/scale
```

- If no improvement, maybe if candidate out of time; adapt? Return False.

If current is at local min, no improvement. Random.

- If line search not improve current, maybe if candidate at alpha small? Return False.

def _smooth_noise(self, scale):
  n=self.n; r=self.rng
  if n<64: g=r.normal(size=n)
  else:
    f=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    spec=r.normal(size=len(f)) + 1j*r.normal(size=len(f))
    cutoff = max(0.02, 0.2? maybe random) 
    spec *= np.exp(-(2*np.pi*f*n)**2/(2*sigma**2))? rfftfreq in cycles/sample, f*2π.
    spec[0]=0; spec[-1]=0
    z=np.fft.irfft(spec, n).real
  z=z-z.mean()
  return scale*z/np.linalg.norm(z) maybe scale on std
```

g=r.normal(size=n)
w=max(3, int(n/20)); k=np.ones(w)/w
z=np.convolve(g,k,mode='same'); z=z-z.mean()
z*=scale/ (np.sqrt(np.mean(z*z))+1e-15)
```

```
def _make_weights(self,b,frac):
  r=self.rng.random()
  rows=b.size; m=b.max(); tau=max(1e-15, frac*m)
  if r < 0.5:
     K=max(1,min(rows,int(round(frac*rows))))
     if K>=rows: idx=slice? 
     idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:] if K<rows else np.arange(rows)
     ex=np.exp((b[idx]-m)/max(1e-15,0.1*tau)) # maybe use tau/5
     vals=np.zeros(rows); vals[idx]=ex; return vals/vals.sum()
  else:
     ex=np.exp((b-m)/tau); return ex/ex.sum()
```

# maybe ensure unique sorted

   pc=softmax(zc); s2,_=self._score(pc)
   if s2<best_s:
      best_s=s2; best_z=zc; best_f=f
if best_f>0:

   return True

   self.frac=min(0.6, self.frac*1.2)
   return False

Smooth noise implementation:
```
def _noise(self, scale=1.0):
 n,n rng
 if n<=32: z=rng.normal(size=n)
 else:
   # smooth via cumulative sum of white noise? Use moving average
   w=max(3, int(n/self.smooth_len)) where smooth_len=3? Actually w=n/15
   g=rng.normal(size=n)
   k=np.ones(w)/w
   z=np.convolve(g,k,mode='same')
 z=z-z.mean()
 norm=math.sqrt(np.mean(z*z)); if norm>0: z=z*scale/norm
 return z
```

SOTA LP in detail with p sum1:

max sum x

def _sota_step(self, p, s, hard_deadline):
 if self.sota_used >= self.sota_max: return False
 if hard_deadline - time < 10: return False
 if self.n > 1024: return False
 try:
   positive p_pos = np.maximum(p, self.lp_floor)
   p_pos/=p_pos.sum()
   bpos = self._convolve(p_pos); M=max(bpos)
   if M<=0: return False
   A=...
   U = min(1000.0, max(20.0, 20.0*M/np.min(p_pos)))
   c=-ones
   res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=M*ones, bounds=[(0,U)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
   if not success return False
   x=res.x; sx=x.sum()
   if sx<1e-8 return False
   x=x/sx
   # optional if max x > 0.9, still okay
   # line search t
   alphas = [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6,0.9,1.0]
   best_p=p; best_s=s
   for t in alphas: if time > hard_deadline-2 break
       q=(1-t)*p + t*x
       if q.min()<0: q=np.maximum(q,0); q/=q.sum()
       s2,_=self._score(q)
       if s2<best_s: best_s=s2; best_p=q
   if best_s < s -1e-12:
       self.z=_logprob(best_p)
       if best_s < self.best_s: update
       self.sota_used +=1
       self.stuck=0
       return True
   self.sota_used +=1
   return False
 except: return False
```

class Job:
  def __init__(self,n,p,seed,use_sota=True):
    self.n=n; self.fs=...; self.rng=...
    p=_as_p(p); self.best_p=p.copy(); self.best_z=_logp(p); z=self.best_z.copy()
    self.best_s, _ = _score_with_b(p, self.fs)
    self.scale=0.3; self.frac=0.12; self.stuck=0; self.it=0
    self.sota_used=0
    self.sota_max = ...
    self.sota_interval = ...
    self.lp_floor = max(1e-8, 1e-4/n)
  def _accept(self,p,s,update):
    p=_as_p(p); s, _ = _score_with_b(p,self.fs) # recompute? already
    if s < self.best_s - 1e-13:
      self.best_p=p.copy(); self.best_s=s; self.best_z=_logp(p)
      if update: update(p,s)
      return True
    return False
  def _score(self,p): return _score_with_b(p,self.fs)
  def _convolve(self,p): F=rfft(p,self.fs); c=irfft(F*F,self.fs); c=c[:2*n-1]; return np.maximum(c,0)
  def _cc(self,q,p): ...
  def _weights(self,b): ...
  def _gradient_step(self,p,b,s,hard): ...
  def _sota_step(self,p,b,s,hard,update): ...
  def run(self,end,hard,update): ...

`_sota_step`:
```
def _sota_step(self,p,b,s,hard,update):
 if linprog is None or self.sota_used>=self.sota_max: return False
 if time.monotonic() > hard - 5: return False
 rows=b.size; mean_p=1.0/self.n
 M = s/(2*self.n) if s>0 else float(b.max())
 if M<=0: return False
 p_pos=np.maximum(p, self.lp_floor); p_pos=p_pos/p_pos.sum()
 # recompute M_pos? using p_pos maybe
 F=rfft(p_pos,self.fs); bpos=irfft(F*F,self.fs)[:rows]; bpos=np.maximum(bpos,0); M=float(bpos.max())
 if M<=0: return False
 A=np.empty((rows,self.n), dtype=float)
 # fill columns
 for i in range(self.n):
    A[i:i+self.n, i] = p_pos
 U = min(500.0, max(5.0, 100.0*M/(1.0/self.n + 1e-15)))
 one=np.ones(self.n)
 try:
   res=linprog(-one, A_ub=A, b_ub=M*np.ones(rows), bounds=[(0.0,U)]*self.n, method='highs')
 except Exception:
   try: res=linprog(... method='simplex')
   except: return False
 if not res.success: self.sota_used+=1; return False
 x=res.x
 sx=float(x.sum())
 if sx<1e-8 or not finite: self.sota_used+=1; return False
 x=x/sx
 # line search
 best_p=p; best_s=s; best_t=0
 for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6,0.9,1.0):
   if time.monotonic() > hard-2: break
   q=(1.0-t)*p + t*x
   q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
   qs=q.sum()
   if qs<=0: continue
   q=q/qs
   q2=np.clip(q,0,1000); qs2=q2.sum(); q2=q2/qs2 # ensure
   s2,_ = self._score(q2)
   if s2 < best_s -1e-13: best_p=q2; best_s=s2; best_t=t

 if best_t>0:

       self.stuck=0; return True
    # if not accept? it should accept if best_s < best_s local; but s current? yes if best_s < self.best_s. If improved current but not local best? `_accept` only local best. We also want accept if best_s < s current but not local best? Since p current maybe worse than local best, s may > local best. SOTA from current could improve current to still not local best; use as current to continue. We can accept as current without updating local:

       self.z=_logp(best_p); return True
 # no improvement
 return False

def _gradient_step(self,p,b,s,hard,update):

`_noise` and `_perturb`:
```
def _noise(self,scale=1.0):
  n=self.n; r=self.rng
  if r.random()<0.5:
    g=r.normal(size=n)
    w=max(3, int(n/15))
    z=np.convolve(g, np.ones(w)/w, mode='same')
  else:
    z=r.normal(size=n)
  z=z-z.mean()
  st=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(z*z)))
  if st>0: z=z*scale/st
  return z
def _perturb(self,z,sigma):
  zc=z.copy()
  r=self.rng.random()
  if r<0.4:
    zc += self._noise(sigma)
  elif r<0.7:
    w=max(1,int(self.rng.integers(1, max(2,n//8))))
    i=self.rng.integers(0,n)
    zc[i:i+w] += sigma*self.rng.normal(size=w)
  else:
    f=1+int(self.rng.integers(0,4)); ph=self.rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)
    zc += sigma*np.sin(2*math.pi*f*np.arange(n)/n + ph)
  zc-=zc.mean()
  return zc
```
Good.

Initial variants code:
```
def _initial_p(n, kind, rng, parent):
 t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
 eps=1e-6
 if kind==0: a=np.ones(n)
 elif kind==1 and parent is not None:
    P=parent.size; idx=np.linspace(0,P-1,n-1) if P>1 else [0]
    a=np.interp(np.arange(n), idx, parent)
 elif kind==2: a=(1+np.arange(n))**(-0.5)
 elif kind==3: a=(1+np.arange(n))[::-1]**(-0.5)
 elif kind==4: a=np.exp(-rng.uniform(0.2,2.5)*t)
 elif kind==5: a=np.exp(-rng.uniform(0.2,2.5)*(1-t))
 elif kind==6: a=np.exp(-rng.uniform(0.1,1.5)*np.minimum(t,1-t)) # center peak? maybe
 elif kind==7:
    a=1.0
    for f in [1,2,3]:
      amp=rng.uniform(0.0,0.7); ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
      a += amp*np.cos(2π f t + ph)
 elif kind==8:
    g=rng.normal(size=n); w=max(3,n//20); a=1+0.8*np.convolve(g,np.ones(w)/w,'same')
 elif kind==9:
    a=np.where(rng.random(n)<0.8, 1.0, 0.2)
 elif kind==10:
    # endpoint block with variable width? p high at end?
    w=max(1,int(rng.integers(n//4, n*3//4)))
    a=np.ones(n); a[:w]*=rng.uniform(0.2,1.0); a[w:]*=rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)
 elif kind==11:
    # inverse sqrt plus oscillation
    base=(1+np.arange(n))**(-0.5); a=base*(1+0.3*np.cos(2π*rng.uniform(1,3)*t))
 else: a=np.ones(n)
 a=np.maximum(a, eps)
 return _as_p(a)
```
Add kind for parent? yes.

Generate initials:
```
def _gen_candidates(n, parent, seed):
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  cands=[]
  for k in range(12):

Detailed code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

def _clean_p(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0])
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0/a.size)
    a = a/s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a

def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0: return np.array([1.0])
    z = z - z.max()
    # clip to avoid under/overflow
    z = np.clip(z, -80.0, 80.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0/z.size)
    p = e/s
    return p

def _logprob(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    floor = 1e-12
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, floor))
    z -= z.mean()
    return z

def _convolve(p, fs):
    F = rfft(p, fs)
    c = irfft(F*F, fs)
    rows = 2*p.size -1
    out = c[:rows].copy()
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out

def _score(p, fs):
    b = _convolve(p, fs)
    m = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0: m=0.0
    return 2.0*p.size*m, b
```
Clipping z to 80 may make floor not exact; okay. If p has one large 0.9, log z -0.1, zeros -27.6? Wait floor1e-12 log -27.6, not -690. Because we use floor1e-12. So zeros become 1e-12 relative, sum n*1e-12 negligible. Good. Clipping -80 fine. This means z range limited; softmax won't underflow. Good.

But floor1e-12 for n=1024 sum mass1e-9, negligible. Good.

Class:
```
class Job:
  def __init__(...):
    self.n=n
    self.fs=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    self.rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    p=_clean_p(p)
    self.best_p=p
    self.best_s,self.best_b=_score(p,self.fs)
    self.best_z=_logprob(p)
    self.z=self.best_z.copy()
    self.scale=0.35
    self.frac=0.12
    self.stuck=0
    self.it=0
    if n <= 160: self.sota_max=400; self.sota_interval=2
    elif n <= 256: self.sota_max=200; self.sota_interval=3
    elif n <= 384: self.sota_max=100; self.sota_interval=5
    elif n <= 512: self.sota_max=60; self.sota_interval=8
    else: self.sota_max=25; self.sota_interval=12
    self.sota_used=0
    self.lp_floor=1e-6
```

`_accept`:
```
  def _accept(self,p,s,update):
    p=_clean_p(p)
    s2,b2=_score(p,self.fs)
    if not math.isfinite(s2): return False
    if s2 < self.best_s - 1e-12:
       self.best_p=p.copy(); self.best_s=s2; self.best_z=_logprob(p)
       if update is not None:
          update(p.copy(), s2)
       return True
    return False
```
This recomputes score, overhead but okay. In line search we already have scores; but ensures consistency.

`_weights`:
```
  def _weights(self,b):
    rows=b.size; m=float(b.max()) if rows else 0.0
    if m<=0: return np.full(rows,1.0/rows)
    frac=max(0.03, min(0.6, self.frac*(1.0 + 0.05*min(self.stuck, 30))))
    tau=max(1e-15, frac*m)
    r=self.rng.random()
    if rows <= 2 or frac > 0.95:
       idx=None
    else:
       K=max(1, min(rows, int(round(frac*rows))))
       if K < rows:
          bno = b + 0.05*tau*self.rng.random(rows)
          idx = np.argpartition(bno, -K)[-K:]
          q=np.zeros(rows); q[idx]=1.0
       else:
          q=np.exp((b-m)/tau)
    if q is None:
       q=np.full(rows,1.0/rows)
    # if top selected, weight by soft within active:
    if K < rows:
       val = np.exp((b[idx]-m)/max(1e-15, 0.1*tau))
       q[idx]=val
    # add small uniform noise
    q += 0.02*self.rng.random(rows)
    q = np.maximum(q,0)
    s=q.sum()
    if s<=0 or not math.isfinite(s): q=np.full(rows,1.0/rows)
    else: q=q/s
    return q
```
But variable K referenced outside; if K>=rows q exp. Need set `top=False`. We'll code cleanly.

`_cc`:

  def _cc(self,q,p):
     fs2=next_fast_len? We didn't store fs2. Store in init: self.fs2=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
     ar=p[::-1]
     c=irfft(rfft(ar,self.fs2)*rfft(q,self.fs2), self.fs2)
     return c[self.n-1:2*self.n-1]
```
Store fs2.

`_noise` and `_perturb`.

`_gradient_step`:
```
  def _gradient_step(self,p,b,s,z,hard,update):
    q=self._weights(b)
    c=self._cc(q,p)
    mean=float(np.dot(p,c))
    u=p*(c-mean)
    umax=float(np.max(np.abs(u))) if u.size else 0.0
    if math.isfinite(umax) and umax>1e-15:
       d=u/umax
    else:
       d=self._noise(1.0)
    if np.max(np.abs(d))>0: d=d/np.max(np.abs(d))
    mults=(0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0)
    best_s=s; best_z=z; best_mult=0.0
    for m in mults:
       a=self.scale*m
       if a <=0: continue
       if time.monotonic() > hard - 0.05: break
       zc=z - a*d
       pc=_softmax(zc)
       s2,_=self._score(pc)
       if s2 < best_s - 1e-13:
          best_s=s2; best_z=zc; best_mult=m
    if best_mult > 0:
       pc=_softmax(best_z)
       # accept local if improved
       improved_local = s2 < self.best_s -1e-12 (use best_s candidate)
       if best_s < self.best_s -1e-12:
           self._accept(pc,best_s,update)
       # adapt scale
       if best_mult == mults[-1]: self.scale=min(2.0, self.scale*1.3)
       elif best_mult == mults[0]: self.scale=max(0.03, self.scale*0.7)
       if best_s < self.best_s: self.frac=max(0.03, self.frac*0.9)
       self.z=best_z
       return True
    else:
       self.frac=min(0.6,self.frac*1.15)
       self.scale=max(0.03,self.scale*0.9)
       return False
```
Need `best_s` for update if candidate local but not new: We compute `pc`. If `best_s < s` return True current. If it also improves local, accept. We need know local_best_after: save old_best=self.best_s. After accept, if local best changed, use `self.best_s`. Return true if best_s < s (current improved) OR local improved. The parameter `best_mult>0` means best_s < s, so return true. Good. If candidate improves local but not current? Since s is current score, local best ≤ s; if candidate < best_s and best_s≤s, then candidate <s, so best_mult>0. Fine.

But if current p is worse than local best, s>best_s. Candidate may improve current but still >best_s; we set z, return true. Good.

Run method:
```
  def run(self,end,hard,update):
    while time.monotonic() < min(end, hard)-0.02:
      self.it +=1
      z=self.z
      p=_softmax(z)
      b,s=self._score(p)
      # if current much worse than best, reset
      if s > self.best_s + max(1e-5, 0.02*self.best_s):
          self.z = self.best_z + self._noise(0.25)
          continue
      improved=False
      # SOTA
      if (self.sota_used < self.sota_max and time.monotonic() < hard - 8 and
          (self.it % self.sota_interval == 0 or self.stuck > 6)):
          if self._sota_step(p,b,s,hard,update):
              improved=True
              self.stuck=0
      if not improved and time.monotonic() < hard - 0.5:
          if self._gradient_step(p,b,s,z,hard,update):
              improved=True
              self.stuck=0
      if not improved:
          # random perturb
          sigma = 0.12 + 0.02*min(self.stuck, 25)
          for _ in range(2):
              if time.monotonic() >= min(end, hard)-0.02: break
              z2=self._perturb(z,sigma)
              p2=_softmax(z2)
              s2,_=self._score(p2)
              if s2 < self.best_s -1e-12:
                  self._accept(p2,s2,update)
                  self.z=z2
                  improved=True
                  self.stuck=0
                  break
              elif s2 < s - 1e-12:
                  self.z=z2
                  # improve current
                  break? We can set improved_current=True but not global. If just current improvement, true to avoid stuck increments? Could count no local.
          if not (improved or (s2 < s)):
              self.stuck +=1
          else:
              # if only current improved, small stuck? maybe not increment
              pass
          if self.stuck > 50:
              self.z = self.best_z + self._noise(0.6)
              self.stuck = 20
          if self.stuck > 100:
              # reset to best
              self.z = self.best_z.copy()
              self.stuck = 0
```

Round-robin function:
```
def _round_robin(jobs,end,hard):
  if not jobs: return
  order=list(range(len(jobs)))
  while time.monotonic() < min(end, hard)-1.0:
    for oi in order:
      if time.monotonic() >= min(end, hard)-0.5: break
      j=jobs[oi]
      slice_sec = 6.0 if j.n <= 256 else 4.0
      tstop = min(end, hard, time.monotonic()+slice_sec)
      try: j.run(tstop, hard, update_best)
      except Exception: pass
    # optional reorder by best_s? do every pass
    # order = sorted(order, key=lambda idx: jobs[idx].best_s)
```

```
def _sota_direction(p, M, fs):
   n=p.size; rows=2n-1
   p_pos=np.maximum(p,1e-6); p_pos/=p_pos.sum()
   bpos=_convolve(p_pos,fs); Mpos=bpos.max()
   if Mpos<=0: return None
   A=np.empty((rows,n)); for i... A[i:i+n,i]=p_pos
   U = min(2000.0, max(10.0, 50.0*Mpos/(Mpos? no mean))) wait use mean=1/n: U=min(2000,max(10,50*Mpos/(1/n+1e-15)))
   try linprog...
   return x_norm

Polish function:
```
def _polish_sota(p, end, hard):
  n=p.size; fs=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
  p=_clean_p(p); s,b=_score(p,fs)
  while time.monotonic() < min(end,hard)-2:
    x=_sota_direction(p, s, fs)
    if x is None: break
    best_p=p; best_s=s
    for t in (0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6,1.0):
       if time.monotonic()>hard-1.5: break
       q=(1-t)*p+t*x; q=np.maximum(q,0); qsum=q.sum(); if qsum<=0: continue; q=q/qsum
       s2,_=self._score? use _score(q,fs)
       if s2<best_s: best_s=s2; best_p=q
    if best_s >= s -1e-13:
       # try smaller t? if none break
       break
    p=best_p; s=best_s
  return p,s
```

def _sota_direction(p, fs, floor=1e-6):
    if linprog is None: return None
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
    n=p.size; rows=2*n-1
    p_pos=np.maximum(p, floor)
    sp=p_pos.sum()
    if sp<=0: return None
    p_pos=p_pos/sp
    bpos=_convolve(p_pos, fs)
    M=float(bpos.max()) if bpos.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M <=0: return None
    A=np.empty((rows,n), dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        A[i:i+n, i] = p_pos
    U = min(2000.0, max(10.0, 50.0 * M / (1.0/n + 1e-15)))
    c=-np.ones(n)
    bv=np.full(rows, M)
    bounds=[(0.0,U)]*n
    try:
       res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=bv, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    except Exception:
       try:
          res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=bv, bounds=bounds)
       except Exception:
          return None
    if res is None or not getattr(res,'success', False): return None
    x=res.x
    x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel()
    if x.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return None
    x=np.maximum(x,0)
    sx=x.sum()
    if sx <=1e-8: return None
    x=x/sx
    x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    sx2=x.sum()
    if sx2<=0: return None
    return x/sx2
```

def _weights(self,b):
 rows=b.size; m=float(b.max())
 if rows==0: return np.array([])
 frac=...
 tau=...
 r=self.rng.random()
 if r < 0.5:
    K=max(1,min(rows,int(round(frac*rows))))
    if K >= rows:
       q=np.exp((b-m)/max(1e-15,tau*5))
    else:
       bnoise=b + 0.05*tau*self.rng.random(rows)
       idx=np.argpartition(bnoise, -K)[-K:]
       q=np.zeros(rows); q[idx]=np.exp((b[idx]-m)/max(1e-15,0.2*tau))
 else:
    q=np.exp((b-m)/tau)
 q +=0.02*self.rng.random(rows)
 q=np.maximum(q,0); s=q.sum(); if s<=0: q=np.full(rows,1/rows); else q/=s
 return q

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


def _clean_p(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    return a


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(z.max())
    z = np.clip(z, -80.0, 80.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _logprob(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-12))
    z -= float(z.mean())
    return z


def _convolve(p, fs):
    F = rfft(p, fs)
    c = irfft(F * F, fs)
    rows = 2 * p.size - 1
    out = c[:rows].copy()
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


def _score(p, fs):
    n = p.size
    b = _convolve(p, fs)
    m = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m), b


def _sota_direction(p, fs):
    """
    One step of the linear-programming best-response construction:
      max 1^T x  s.t. x >= 0 and p * x <= M,
    where M = ||p*p||_infty.  Returns x normalized to sum 1, or None.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    rows = 2 * n - 1

    floor = max(1e-8, 1e-4 / n)
    p_pos = np.maximum(p, floor)
    sp = float(p_pos.sum())
    if sp <= 1e-12:
        return None
    p_pos = p_pos / sp

    b_pos = _convolve(p_pos, fs)
    M = float(b_pos.max()) if b_pos.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    # A[k, i] = p_pos[k-i] if 0 <= k-i < n
    A = np.empty((rows, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        A[i:i + n, i] = p_pos

    # A moderately large upper bound keeps the LP well-scaled after rescaling.
    mean_p = 1.0 / n
    U = min(2000.0, max(10.0, 50.0 * M / (mean_p + 1e-15)))
    cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bvec = np.full(rows, M, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, float(U))] * n

    try:
        res = linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=bounds)
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None

    x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return None
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    sx = float(x.sum())
    if sx <= 1e-8:
        return None
    x = x / sx
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    sx = float(x.sum())
    if sx <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return x / sx


class Job:
    def __init__(self, n, p, seed):
        self.n = int(n)
        self.fs = next_fast_len(2 * self.n - 1)
        self.fs2 = next_fast_len(3 * self.n - 2)
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

        p = _clean_p(p)
        self.best_p = p.copy()
        self.best_s, _ = _score(p, self.fs)
        self.best_z = _logprob(p)
        self.z = self.best_z.copy()

        self.scale = 0.35
        self.frac = 0.12
        self.stuck = 0
        self.it = 0

        if self.n <= 160:
            self.sota_max = 500
            self.sota_interval = 2
        elif self.n <= 256:
            self.sota_max = 250
            self.sota_interval = 3
        elif self.n <= 384:
            self.sota_max = 120
            self.sota_interval = 5
        elif self.n <= 512:
            self.sota_max = 60
            self.sota_interval = 8
        else:
            self.sota_max = 24
            self.sota_interval = 12
        self.sota_used = 0

    def _accept(self, p, s, update):
        p = _clean_p(p)
        s2, _ = _score(p, self.fs)
        if not math.isfinite(s2):
            return False
        if s2 < self.best_s - 1e-12:
            self.best_p = p.copy()
            self.best_s = float(s2)
            self.best_z = _logprob(p)
            if update is not None:
                try:
                    update(p.copy(), float(s2))
                except Exception:
                    pass
            return True
        return False

    def _cc(self, q, p):
        ar = p[::-1]
        c = irfft(rfft(ar, self.fs2) * rfft(q, self.fs2), self.fs2)
        return c[self.n - 1:2 * self.n - 1]

    def _weights(self, b):
        rows = b.size
        m = float(b.max()) if rows else 0.0
        if rows <= 0 or m <= 0.0:
            return np.ones(max(rows, 1), dtype=np.float64) / max(rows, 1)

        frac = max(0.03, min(0.6, self.frac * (1.0 + 0.05 * min(self.stuck, 30))))
        tau = max(1e-15, frac * m)

        if self.rng.random() < 0.5:
            K = max(1, min(rows, int(round(frac * rows))))
            if K >= rows:
                q = np.exp((b - m) / max(1e-15, 5.0 * tau))
            else:
                bnoise = b + 0.05 * tau * self.rng.random(rows)
                idx = np.argpartition(bnoise, -K)[-K:]
                q = np.zeros(rows, dtype=np.float64)
                q[idx] = np.exp((b[idx] - m) / max(1e-15, 0.2 * tau))
        else:
            q = np.exp((b - m) / tau)

        q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64)
        q += 0.02 * self.rng.random(rows)
        q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
        qs = float(q.sum())
        if qs <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(qs):
            q = np.full(rows, 1.0 / rows, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            q = q / qs
        return q

    def _noise(self, scale=1.0):
        n = self.n
        r = self.rng
        if n <= 32 or r.random() < 0.3:
            z = r.normal(size=n)
        else:
            g = r.normal(size=n)
            w = max(3, int(n // 15))
            z = np.convolve(g, np.ones(w) / w, mode="same")[:n]
        z = z - z.mean()
        st = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(z * z))) if z.size else 0.0
        if st > 1e-15:
            z = z * (float(scale) / st)
        return z

    def _perturb(self, z, sigma):
        n = self.n
        zc = z.copy()
        r = self.rng.random()
        if r < 0.45:
            zc += self._noise(sigma)
        elif r < 0.70:
            w = max(1, int(r.integers(1, max(2, n // 8))))
            i = int(r.integers(0, n))
            zc[i:i + w] += float(sigma) * r.normal(size=w)
        else:
            f = 1 + int(r.integers(0, 4))
            ph = float(r.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            x = np.arange(n) / max(1, n)
            zc += float(sigma) * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * x + ph)
        zc -= zc.mean()
        return zc

    def _gradient_step(self, p, b, s, z, hard, update):
        q = self._weights(b)
        c = self._cc(q, p)
        mean_c = float(np.dot(p, c))
        u = p * (c - mean_c)
        umax = float(np.max(np.abs(u))) if u.size else 0.0
        if math.isfinite(umax) and umax > 1e-15:
            d = u / umax
        else:
            d = self._noise(1.0)
            dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(d))) if d.size else 0.0
            if dmax > 1e-15:
                d = d / dmax

        mults = (0.05, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 1.00, 2.00)
        best_s = s
        best_z = z
        best_pc = p
        best_mult = 0.0

        for m in mults:
            a = self.scale * float(m)
            if a <= 0.0:
                continue
            if time.monotonic() > hard - 0.05:
                break
            zc = z - a * d
            pc = _softmax(zc)
            s2, _ = self._score(pc, self.fs)
            if s2 < best_s - 1e-13:
                best_s = s2
                best_z = zc
                best_pc = pc
                best_mult = float(m)

        if best_mult > 0.0:
            if best_s < self.best_s - 1e-12:
                self._accept(best_pc, best_s, update)

            if best_mult == mults[-1]:
                self.scale = min(2.0, self.scale * 1.25)
            elif best_mult == mults[0]:
                self.scale = max(0.03, self.scale * 0.75)

            if best_s < self.best_s:
                self.frac = max(0.03, self.frac * 0.9)
            else:
                self.frac = min(0.6, self.frac * 1.05)

            self.z = best_z
            return True

        self.frac = min(0.6, self.frac * 1.15)
        self.scale = max(0.03, self.scale * 0.9)
        return False

    def _sota_step(self, p, s, hard, update):
        if self.sota_used >= self.sota_max:
            return False
        if time.monotonic() > hard - 4.0:
            return False

        x = _sota_direction(p, self.fs)
        self.sota_used += 1
        if x is None:
            return False

        best_p = p
        best_s = s
        best_t = 0.0

        for t in (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.60, 0.90, 1.00):
            if time.monotonic() > hard - 1.0:
                break
            q = (1.0 - t) * p + t * x
            q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
            qs = float(q.sum())
            if qs <= 1e-12:
                continue
            q = q / qs
            s2, _ = self._score(q, self.fs)
            if s2 < best_s - 1e-13:
                best_s = s2
                best_p = q
                best_t = float(t)

        if best_t > 0.0:
            improved_local = self._accept(best_p, best_s, update)
            if not improved_local:
                self.z = _logprob(best_p)
            return True

        return False

    def run(self, end, hard, update):
        stop = min(float(end), float(hard)) - 0.02
        while time.monotonic() < stop:
            self.it += 1
            z = self.z
            p = _softmax(z)
            b, s = _score(p, self.fs)

            # If the working point drifted far below the local best, reset.
            if s > self.best_s + max(1e-5, 0.02 * self.best_s):
                self.z = self.best_z + self._noise(0.30)
                continue

            improved = False

            if (self.sota_used < self.sota_max and
                    time.monotonic() < hard - 8.0 and
                    (self.it % self.sota_interval == 0 or self.stuck > 6)):
                if self._sota_step(p, s, hard, update):
                    improved = True
                    self.stuck = 0

            if not improved and time.monotonic() < hard - 0.5:
                if self._gradient_step(p, b, s, z, hard, update):
                    improved = True
                    self.stuck = 0

            if not improved:
                sigma = 0.12 + 0.02 * min(self.stuck, 25)
                moved_current = False
                for _ in range(2):
                    if time.monotonic() >= stop:
                        break
                    z2 = self._perturb(z, sigma)
                    p2 = _softmax(z2)
                    s2, _ = _score(p2, self.fs)
                    if s2 < self.best_s - 1e-12:
                        self._accept(p2, s2, update)
                        self.z = z2
                        improved = True
                        self.stuck = 0
                        break
                    if s2 < s - 1e-12:
                        self.z = z2
                        moved_current = True

                if not improved:
                    if moved_current:
                        self.stuck = max(0, self.stuck - 1)
                    else:
                        self.stuck += 1

                if self.stuck > 50:
                    self.z = self.best_z + self._noise(0.60)
                    self.stuck = 20
                if self.stuck > 100:
                    self.z = self.best_z.copy()
                    self.stuck = 0


def _initial_p(n, kind, rng, parent):
    n = max(2, int(n))
    t = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    eps = 1e-6

    if kind == 0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif kind == 1 and parent is not None:
        P = int(parent.size)
        if P <= 0:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        elif P == 1:
            a = np.full(n, float(parent[0]))
        else:
            idx = np.linspace(0.0, P - 1.0, n)
            a = np.interp(np.arange(n), idx, parent)
    elif kind == 2:
        a = (1.0 + np.arange(n)) ** (-0.5)
    elif kind == 3:
        a = (1.0 + np.arange(n))[::-1] ** (-0.5)
    elif kind == 4:
        a = np.exp(-float(rng.uniform(0.20, 2.50)) * t)
    elif kind == 5:
        a = np.exp(-float(rng.uniform(0.20, 2.50)) * (1.0 - t))
    elif kind == 6:
        a = np.exp(-float(rng.uniform(0.10, 1.50)) * np.minimum(t, 1.0 - t))
    elif kind == 7:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for f in (1, 2, 3):
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.70))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
            a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * t + ph)
    elif kind == 8:
        g = rng.normal(size=n)
        w = max(3, int(n // 20))
        a = 1.0 + 0.8 * np.convolve(g, np.ones(w) / w, mode="same")[:n]
    elif kind == 9:
        keep = rng.random(n) < 0.80
        a = np.where(keep, 1.0, 0.20)
    elif kind == 10:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        w = max(1, int(rng.integers(n // 4, max(2, n * 3 // 4))))
        a[:w] *= float(rng.uniform(0.20, 1.0))
        a[w:] *= float(rng.uniform(0.20, 1.0))
    elif kind == 11:
        base = (1.0 + np.arange(n)) ** (-0.5)
        f = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 3.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        a = base * (1.0 + 0.30 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * t + ph))
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, eps)
    return _clean_p(a)


def _make_focus_perturb(p, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    p = _clean_p(p)
    z = _logprob(p)
    g = rng.normal(size=p.size)
    w = max(3, int(p.size // 15))
    nz = np.convolve(g, np.ones(w) / w, mode="same")[:p.size]
    nz -= nz.mean()
    st = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(nz * nz))) if nz.size else 0.0
    if st > 1e-15:
        nz = nz / st
    q = _softmax(z + 0.25 * nz)
    return _clean_p(q)


def _polish_sota(p, end, hard):
    p = _clean_p(p)
    n = p.size
    fs = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
    s, _ = _score(p, fs)

    while time.monotonic() < min(float(end), float(hard)) - 2.0:
        x = _sota_direction(p, fs)
        if x is None:
            break

        best_p = p
        best_s = s
        for t in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.60, 1.00):
            if time.monotonic() > hard - 1.0:
                break
            q = (1.0 - t) * p + t * x
            q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
            qs = float(q.sum())
            if qs <= 1e-12:
                continue
            q = q / qs
            s2, _ = _score(q, fs)
            if s2 < best_s - 1e-13:
                best_s = s2
                best_p = q

        if best_s >= s - 1e-13:
            break
        p = best_p
        s = best_s

    return p, s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    hard = t0 + total - 6.0

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            hp = _clean_p(h)
            if hp.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(hp)):
                parent = hp
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    def _init_score(a):
        a = _clean_p(a)
        fs = next_fast_len(2 * a.size - 1)
        s, _ = _score(a, fs)
        return a, float(s)

    if parent is not None:
        best_p, best_s = _init_score(parent)
    else:
        best_p = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        best_s = 2.0

    if not math.isfinite(best_s):
        best_p = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        best_s = 2.0

    best = {"p": best_p, "s": float(best_s)}

    def update_best(p, s):
        try:
            p = _clean_p(p)
            s = float(s)
            if math.isfinite(s) and s < best["s"] - 1e-12:
                best["p"] = p.copy()
                best["s"] = s
        except Exception:
            pass

    update_best(best_p, best_s)

    lengths = []
    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 32:
        lengths.append(parent.size)
    for n in (128, 160, 192, 256, 384, 512):
        lengths.append(n)

    unique_lengths = []
    for n in lengths:
        n = int(n)
        if 32 <= n <= 512 and n not in unique_lengths:
            unique_lengths.append(n)

    seed = int(seed)
    all_cands = []

    # Quick batch of candidate shapes across selected lengths.
    for n in unique_lengths:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed * 1000003 + n * 17 + 7)
        for k in range(12):
            try:
                p = _initial_p(n, k, rng, parent)
                fs = next_fast_len(2 * n - 1)
                s, _ = _score(p, fs)
                if math.isfinite(s):
                    all_cands.append((float(s), p))
                    update_best(p, s)
            except Exception:
                pass

    all_cands.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

    jobs = []
    count_by_len = {}

    if parent is not None:
        try:
            jobs.append(Job(parent.size, parent, seed + 1))
            count_by_len[parent.size] = count_by_len.get(parent.size, 0) + 1
        except Exception:
            pass

    for s, p in all_cands:
        if len(jobs) >= 14:
            break
        n = int(p.size)
        if count_by_len.get(n, 0) >= 3:
            continue
        try:
            jobs.append(Job(n, p, seed + 100 + len(jobs)))
            count_by_len[n] = count_by_len.get(n, 0) + 1
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Make sure the current global best also has a dedicated job.
    try:
        best_len = int(best["p"].size)
        if count_by_len.get(best_len, 0) < 3:
            jobs.append(Job(best_len, best["p"], seed + 300 + len(jobs)))
            count_by_len[best_len] = count_by_len.get(best_len, 0) + 1
    except Exception:
        pass

    def round_robin(job_list, end, hard_limit):
        if not job_list:
            return
        order = list(range(len(job_list)))
        while time.monotonic() < min(float(end), float(hard_limit)) - 1.0:
            for oi in order:
                if time.monotonic() >= min(float(end), float(hard_limit)) - 0.5:
                    break
                j = job_list[oi]
                slice_sec = 6.0 if j.n <= 256 else 4.0
                tstop = min(float(end), float(hard_limit), time.monotonic() + slice_sec)
                try:
                    j.run(tstop, hard_limit, update_best)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            order.sort(key=lambda idx: job_list[idx].best_s)

    usable = max(0.0, hard - t0)

    if usable > 30.0:
        final_polish = min(max(30.0, 0.10 * usable), 120.0)
        phase2_end = hard - max(5.0, final_polish)
        phase1_end = t0 + min(
            0.70 * max(0.0, phase2_end - t0),
            max(20.0, usable - max(60.0, final_polish) - 20.0)
        )
        phase1_end = min(phase1_end, phase2_end)

        if phase1_end > t0 + 5.0:
            round_robin(jobs, phase1_end, hard)

        if time.monotonic() < phase2_end - 5.0:
            focus = []
            persistent_jobs = sorted(jobs, key=lambda j: j.best_s)
            for j in persistent_jobs[:4]:
                try:
                    focus.append(Job(j.n, j.best_p, seed + 1000 + len(focus)))
                except Exception:
                    pass
            try:
                focus.append(Job(best["p"].size, _make_focus_perturb(best["p"], seed + 31415), seed + 2000 + len(focus)))
            except Exception:
                pass
            focus.sort(key=lambda j: j.best_s)
            if focus:
                round_robin(focus, phase2_end, hard)

        # Final dedicated SOTA polish on the best exact-scoring candidate.
        if time.monotonic() < hard - 5.0:
            try:
                pp, ss = _polish_sota(best["p"], hard - 2.0, hard)
                update_best(pp, ss)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Final exact validation and safe return.
    def _valid_candidate_sequence(p):
        try:
            p = _clean_p(p)
            cand = [float(v) for v in p]
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(cand))
            if math.isfinite(sc):
                return cand, sc
        except Exception:
            pass
        return None, math.inf

    cand, sc = _valid_candidate_sequence(best["p"])
    if cand is not None:
        return cand

    if parent is not None:
        cand, sc = _valid_candidate_sequence(parent)
        if cand is not None:
            return cand

    fallback = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0
    cand, sc = _valid_candidate_sequence(fallback)
    if cand is not None:
        return cand

    return [1.0]
```
